Local plane k (1..lnz) corresponds to global plane z0+k-1.\n// Global boundary points are copied through unchanged.\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,\n                              Real* __restrict__ output,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const size_t lnz, const size_t z0) {\n    const size_t x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t k = blockIdx.z * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + 1; k <= lnz; ++k) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t gz = z0 + k - 1;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t gidx = idx3(x, y, gz, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx3(x, y, k, nx, ny)] = (gidx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool hasBad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(||: hasBad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        hasBad = hasBad || std::isnan(grid[i]) || std::isinf(grid[i]);\n    }\n    if (hasBad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min: minVal) reduction(max: maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\\n\", nranks, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // Bind each rank to a GPU (round-robin over devices visible on the node)\n    {\n        MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,\n                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n        int localRank = 0;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, The stencil3d benchmark is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program, verified bit-exact against the original serial code and reaching **68.4 GCUPS on 4 GPUs at 768³** (vs 19.8 on one GPU, 3.45× scaling; the ~360 GB/s-per-GPU memory-bound kernel is the limit).\n\n**Design** (all in the existing `stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt`, executable name unchanged):\n\n- **MPI**: 1D domain decomposition into contiguous Z-slabs, one rank per GPU (round-robin binding via a shared-memory sub-communicator, so it works across nodes of a cluster). Each iteration exchanges one halo plane with each Z-neighbor via `MPI_Sendrecv` through pinned host buffers. Uneven divisions and more ranks than planes are handled (surplus ranks idle through the collectives).\n- **CUDA**: the 7-point stencil runs as a kernel on double-buffered device arrays that stay resident for all iterations; global-boundary points are copied through in-kernel, matching the original semantics. Communication is overlapped with computation using two streams — interior planes compute on one stream while boundary planes are staged, exchanged over MPI, and their edge-plane kernels run on the other. This overlap roughly doubled multi-GPU throughput (31 → 58 GCUPS at 512³).\n- **OpenMP**: parallelizes host-side work — grid initialization (each rank initializes its slab with the exact original values via global indices) and the validation reductions on the gathered result.\n- **Numerics**: the kernel uses the same operand ordering as the original expression, so results are bit-identical — verified via the result hash across grid shapes (64³, 65×33×47, 16×16×5, 512³), even/odd/zero iteration counts, and 1/2/3/4/8 ranks, all matching the serial reference exactly.\n\n**CMakeLists** enables the CUDA language (compiling the existing `.cpp` as CUDA via a source property, so no new files), links `MPI::MPI_CXX` and `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`, targets sm_86 by default (overridable via `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES`), and selects `g++-12` as the nvcc host compiler since CUDA 12.0 doesn't support the system's default GCC 13.\n\nRun with e.g. `mpirun -np 4 ./stencil3d -x 512 -i 50 -v`."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":261,"cache_read_input_tokens":39528,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":261},"output_tokens":780,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"85c018e8-68fb-499b-8dd6-c01618f858de","uuid":"42b8de32-b979-47cb-bb51-e3ba87d49b59","timestamp":"2026-09-04T20:22:57.582Z","request_id":"req_011Cej2HPzjTbC6gRStd527D"}

Each iteration exchanges one halo plane with each Z-neighbor via `MPI_Sendrecv` through pinned host buffers. Uneven divisions and more ranks than planes are handled (surplus ranks idle through the collectives).\n- **CUDA**: the 7-point stencil runs as a kernel on double-buffered device arrays that stay resident for all iterations; global-boundary points are copied through in-kernel, matching the original semantics. Communication is overlapped with computation using two streams — interior planes compute on one stream while boundary planes are staged, exchanged over MPI, and their edge-plane kernels run on the other. This overlap roughly doubled multi-GPU throughput (31 → 58 GCUPS at 512³).\n- **OpenMP**: parallelizes host-side work — grid initialization (each rank initializes its slab with the exact original values via global indices) and the validation reductions on the gathered result.\n- **Numerics**: the kernel uses the same operand ordering as the original expression, so results are bit-identical — verified via the result hash across grid shapes (64³, 65×33×47, 16×16×5, 512³), even/odd/zero iteration counts, and 1/2/3/4/8 ranks, all matching the serial reference exactly.\n\n**CMakeLists** enables the CUDA language (compiling the existing `.cpp` as CUDA via a source property, so no new files), links `MPI::MPI_CXX` and `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`, targets sm_86 by default (overridable via `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES`), and selects `g++-12` as the nvcc host compiler since CUDA 12.0 doesn't support the system's default GCC 13.\n\nRun with e.g. `mpirun -np 4 ./stencil3d -x 512 -i 50 -v`.","ttft_ms":3426,"ttft_stream_ms":2689,"time_to_request_ms":68,"type":"result","duration_ms":611763,"uuid":"3f7a8eff-6dcd-474d-a119-0226dee7436a","queued_turn_count":0}
